##### Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: person.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}

  - id: person.sav_excess
    printed: "(screen)"
    label: "Form 8880, line 1: IRA contributions for 2025 that may include an excess contribution left in the IRA"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_owner, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_extra_left, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_excess, {const: true}]}
    note: "Open: the Form 8880 text does not say how an excess IRA contribution that stays in the IRA is counted on line 1. Chosen reading: the screen is true when the filer reports an unwithdrawn excess contribution, or when the IRA deduction worksheet's item 10 condition (person.ira_excess) holds."

  - id: person.ira_excess
    printed: "(screen)"
    label: "If the total of your IRA deduction on line 20 plus any nondeductible contributions to your traditional IRAs shown on Form 8606 is less than your total traditional IRA contributions for 2025, see Pub. 590-A for special rules"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [person.h_ira_ded_plus_nd, person.ira_contrib]}
    note: "Chosen reading: the screen is item 10 of the list in the line 20 instructions (deduction plus nondeductible contributions less than the total traditional IRA contributions)."

  - id: person.h_ira_ded_plus_nd
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA deduction plus nondeductible traditional IRA contributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.ira_deduction, person.ira_nondeductible]}

  # ---------------- Ages ----------------
  - id: person.birth_year_bad
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Birth year outside the accepted range"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [person.birth_year, {param: age.birth_year_earliest}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [person.birth_year, {param: age.birth_year_latest}]}

  - id: person.age65
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Born before January 2, 1961"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_65}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_65}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [person.born_jan1, {const: true}]}

  - id: person.died_before_65
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Born before January 2, 1961, but died in 2025 before reaching age 65"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.age65, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.died_at_64, {const: true}]}

  - id: person.age65_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 12d box: born before January 2, 1961 (not checked for a person who died in 2025 before reaching age 65)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.age65, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.died_at_64, {const: false}]}

  - id: person.under24
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Under age 24 at the end of 2025 (born after January 1, 2002)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_24}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_24}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [person.born_jan1, {const: false}]}

  - id: person.under16
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Under age 16 at the end of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_16}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_16}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [person.born_jan1, {const: false}]}
    note: "Computed from the birth year with the rule that a person reaches an age on the day before the birthday (born January 1, 2010 is age 16 at the end of 2025); the input person.under16_at_year_end is not used."

  - id: person.h_under18
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Under age 18 at the end of 2025 (born after January 1, 2008)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_18}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_18}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [person.born_jan1, {const: false}]}

  - id: person.h_under19
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Under age 19 at the end of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_19}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_19}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [person.born_jan1, {const: false}]}

  - id: person.eic_age25
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "At least age 25 at the end of 2025 (born before January 2, 2001)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_25}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_25}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [person.born_jan1, {const: true}]}

  - id: person.eic_under65
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Under age 65 at the end of 2025 (born after December 31, 1960)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, ge: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_65}]}
    note: "Open: the EIC Step 4 question 2 says to check Yes if born after December 31, 1960, which includes January 1, 1961, although such a person reaches 65 on December 31, 2025. Chosen: follow the stated date test literally."

  - id: person.ira_age50
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Age 50 or older at the end of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_50}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_50}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [person.born_jan1, {const: true}]}
    note: "Computed from the birth year with the day-before-birthday rule; the input person.age50_at_year_end is not used."

  - id: person.age55_at_year_end_if_open_year
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Age 55 or older at the end of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_55}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_55}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [person.born_jan1, {const: true}]}
    note: "Open: the pages do not say what 'open year' qualifies. Chosen: age 55 or older at the end of 2025 from the birth year (born before January 2, 1971), the condition for the Form 8889 additional $1,000 contribution."

  # ---------------- Educator expenses ----------------
  - id: person.h_educator_max
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Educator expense limit per person ($300)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: s1.educator_max}

  - id: person.h_educator_capped
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualified educator expenses, up to $300"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.educator_expenses, person.h_educator_max]}

  - id: person.educator_deduction
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule 1, line 11: educator expenses (up to $300 of qualified expenses for an eligible educator)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.eligible_educator, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_educator_capped}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.eligible_educator, {const: false}]}, then: person.h_zero}

  # ---------------- Tips ----------------
  - id: person.tip_reported
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualified tips reported by each employer (Qualified Tips From More Than One Employer Worksheet, column (b)), total"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: tipjob, field: tipjob.amount}
    note: "Chosen: column (b) of the worksheet; column (c), Form 4137 tips, is outside the tool (screened by person.se_screen)."

  # ---------------- SEP / SIMPLE (Schedule 1, line 16) ----------------
  - id: person.h_sep_ded
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SEP: actual contribution, not more than the maximum deductible contribution (step 21)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.sep_contrib, person.sep_s21]}

  - id: person.h_simple_profit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Net profit, if more than zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.sep_s1, take: person.h_zero}

  - id: person.h_simple_comp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SIMPLE compensation: net earnings from self-employment (Schedule SE, line 4) before SIMPLE contributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_simple_profit, rate: {param: se.rate_9235}}
    note: "Pub. 560 defines SIMPLE compensation as Schedule SE line 4. Chosen: 92.35% of the positive net profit (step 1); the $400 rule of line 4c and the one-business-with-a-plan rule are not applied."

  - id: person.h_simple_catchup
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SIMPLE catch-up contribution limit for this age"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: keyed_lookup, table: simple_catchup, keys: [person.simple_age_at_year_end]}

  - id: person.h_simple_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SIMPLE salary reduction limit ($16,500)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ret.simple_limit}

  - id: person.h_simple_deferral_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Salary reduction limit plus catch-up limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_simple_limit, person.h_simple_catchup]}

  - id: person.h_simple_def_cap1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Salary reduction contributions, up to the limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.simple_deferral, person.h_simple_deferral_limit]}

  - id: person.h_simple_def_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Salary reduction contributions, not more than compensation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_simple_def_cap1, person.h_simple_comp]}

  - id: person.h_simple_match
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "3% of compensation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_simple_comp, rate: {param: ret.match_rate}}

  - id: person.h_simple_match_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Matching contribution: dollar for dollar up to 3% of compensation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_simple_match, person.h_simple_def_ok]}

  - id: person.h_ret_comp_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Compensation limit ($350,000)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ret.comp_limit}

  - id: person.h_simple_comp_capped
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Compensation, up to $350,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_simple_comp, person.h_ret_comp_limit]}

  - id: person.h_simple_nonelective
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "2% nonelective contribution"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_simple_comp_capped, rate: {param: ret.nonelective_rate}}
    note: "The $5,000 (or lower) compensation condition for nonelective contributions is not applied."

  - id: person.h_simple_emp_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Employer contribution allowed by the plan formula"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.simple_kind, {const: "match"}]}, then: person.h_simple_match_cap}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.simple_kind, {const: "nonelective"}]}, then: person.h_simple_nonelective}

  - id: person.h_simple_emp_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Employer contributions made, up to the formula amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.simple_employer, person.h_simple_emp_cap]}

  - id: person.h_simple_ded
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SIMPLE IRA contributions for yourself"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_simple_def_ok, person.h_simple_emp_ok]}

  - id: person.h_plan_refused
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Plan situation outside the tool"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, ne: [person.plan, {const: "none"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.plan_screen, {const: true}]}

  - id: person.s16
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule 1, line 16: self-employed SEP and SIMPLE plans (actual contribution, not more than the maximum deductible contribution)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "sep"}]}, then: person.h_sep_ded}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "simple"}]}, then: person.h_simple_ded}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "none"}]}, then: person.h_zero}
    refuses: [person.h_plan_refused]

  # ---------------- Saver's credit ----------------
  - id: person.sav_ineligible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8880: person born after January 1, 2008, claimed as a dependent on someone else's 2025 return, or a student"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.h_under18, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_student, {const: true}]}

  - id: person.sav_roth_if_ira_owner
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Roth IRA contributions for 2025 (zero if no IRA)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_owner, {const: true}]}, then: person.sav_roth}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_owner, {const: false}]}, then: person.h_zero}

  - id: person.h_rechar_refused
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Recharacterized contribution"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_owner, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_recharacterized, {const: true}]}

  - id: person.ira_contrib
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Traditional IRA contributions for 2025 (IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 11)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_owner, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_trad, {const: true}]}
          then: person.ira_trad
        - guard:
            op: test
            not:
              op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_owner, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_trad, {const: true}]}
          then: person.h_zero
    refuses: [person.h_rechar_refused]

  - id: person.sav_line1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8880, line 1: traditional and Roth IRA contributions for 2025 (not rollovers)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.ira_contrib, person.sav_roth_if_ira_owner]}
    refuses: [person.sav_excess, person.sav_able]

  - id: person.h_w2_sav
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Elective deferrals shown on Forms W-2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.sav_deferral}

  - id: person.h_sav_simple
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SIMPLE salary reduction contributions for yourself as self-employed"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "simple"}]}, then: person.simple_deferral}
        - {guard: {op: test, ne: [person.plan, {const: "simple"}]}, then: person.h_zero}

  - id: person.sav_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8880, line 2: elective deferrals, voluntary employee contributions and 501(c)(18)(D) contributions for 2025"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_w2_sav, person.sav_voluntary, person.h_sav_simple]}

  # ---------------- IRA deduction worksheet, line 7 ----------------
  - id: person.h_ira_limit_base
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$7,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.limit}

  - id: person.h_ira_limit_50
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$8,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.limit_50}

  - id: person.h_ira_lim
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$7,000 if under age 50 at the end of 2025; $8,000 if age 50 or older"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_ira_limit_50}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: false}]}, then: person.h_ira_limit_base}

  - id: person.h_line6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet line 6, this person's column"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.role, {const: "taxpayer"}]}, then: ira.line6a}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.role, {const: "spouse"}]}, then: ira.line6b}

  - id: person.h_narrow
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Column uses 70%/80% and $10,000 (single, HOH, MFS, or the column of a person not covered on a joint return)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "single"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_work_plan, {const: false}]}

  - id: person.h_p70
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 × 70%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_70}}

  - id: person.h_p80
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 × 80%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_80}}

  - id: person.h_p35
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 × 35%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_35}}

  - id: person.h_p40
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 × 40%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_40}}

  - id: person.h_p_narrow
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 × 70% (80% if age 50 or older)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_p80}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: false}]}, then: person.h_p70}

  - id: person.h_p_wide
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 × 35% (40% if age 50 or older)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_p40}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: false}]}, then: person.h_p35}

  - id: person.h_prod
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 multiplied by the applicable percentage"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_narrow, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_p_narrow}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_narrow, {const: false}]}, then: person.h_p_wide}

  - id: person.h_prod_up
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Increased to the next multiple of $10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: round, of: person.h_prod, unit: 1000, rule: up}

  - id: person.h_ira_min
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$200"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.min}

  - id: person.h_prod_min
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "If less than $200, enter $200"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: larger_of, of: [person.h_prod_up, person.h_ira_min]}

  - id: person.h_full_narrow
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$10,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.full_narrow}

  - id: person.h_full_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$20,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.full_joint}

  - id: person.h_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 amount at or above which the full limit applies"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_narrow, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_full_narrow}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_narrow, {const: false}]}, then: person.h_full_joint}

  - id: person.ira_line7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 7 (this person's column)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [ira.lines2to6, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_lim
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [ira.lines2to6, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.h_line6, {const: 0}]}
          then: person.h_zero
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [ira.lines2to6, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, gt: [person.h_line6, {const: 0}]}
              - {op: test, ge: [person.h_line6, person.h_threshold]}
          then: person.h_ira_lim
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [ira.lines2to6, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, gt: [person.h_line6, {const: 0}]}
              - {op: test, lt: [person.h_line6, person.h_threshold]}
          then: person.h_prod_min
    note: "A line 6 of zero is read as the line 6 'No' STOP (none of the contributions deductible), giving zero rather than the $200 minimum."

  - id: person.h_ded_7_10
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of line 7 and line 10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.ira_line7, ira.line10]}

  - id: person.ira_deduction
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 12: the smallest of line 7, 10 or 11 (this person's column)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_ded_7_10, person.ira_contrib]}

  - id: person.h_nd_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of worksheet line 10 or line 11"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [ira.line10, person.ira_contrib]}

  - id: person.ira_nondeductible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606, line 1: smaller of worksheet line 10 or line 11, minus line 12"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_nd_cap, take: person.ira_deduction}

  - id: person.h_ssb_7_10
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Contribution limit, not more than line 10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_ira_lim, ira.line10]}

  - id: person.ira_ssb
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA deduction where lines 2 through 6 do not apply (used by the Social Security Benefits Worksheet)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_ssb_7_10, person.ira_contrib]}
    note: "The Social Security Benefits Worksheet may not be used when traditional IRA contributions are made and anyone is covered by a plan. Chosen: where it is used, the IRA deduction does not depend on income, so it is the smallest of the $7,000/$8,000 limit, line 10 and line 11."

  - id: person.ira_se
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 9 part: net earnings from self-employment minus Schedule 1, lines 15 and 16"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.sep_s3, take: person.s16}
    note: "Uses step 3 (net profit less the self-employment tax deduction) and floors each person at zero; the worksheet floors the combined amount."

  # ---------------- Form 8606 ----------------
  - id: person.h_basis_refused
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Basis changed other than by nondeductible contributions"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_owner, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_basis_adjusted, {const: true}]}

  - id: person.ira_8606_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606, line 2: total basis in traditional IRAs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_owner, {const: true}]}, then: person.ira_basis}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_owner, {const: false}]}, then: person.h_zero}
    refuses: [person.h_basis_refused]

  - id: person.ira_dist_gate
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Distribution or conversion from a traditional IRA with basis (Form 8606 lines 4 to 13 required)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_owner, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_dist, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, gt: [person.ira_nondeductible, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [person.ira_8606_line2, {const: 0}]}
    note: "Chosen reading: true when Form 8606 Part I's 'Yes' path (line 4 onward) applies."

  - id: person.ira_files_8606
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Must file Form 8606 (nondeductible contributions for 2025, or a distribution from traditional IRAs with basis)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [person.ira_nondeductible, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_dist_gate, {const: true}]}
    note: "Only the Part I filing conditions are encoded; Roth conversions without basis and Roth distributions are not."

  # ---------------- Education credits (Form 8863 Part III) ----------------
  - id: person.h_edu_base
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Education expenses paid, Form 1098-T requirement met, and TIN issued by the due date"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.tin, {const: true}]}

  - id: person.h_edu_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lines 23 to 26 lead to lines 27 through 30 (American opportunity credit)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_base, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line23, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line24, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line25, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line26, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_ein, {const: true}]}
    note: "Without the institution's EIN the American opportunity credit cannot be claimed; chosen: the student then goes to line 31."

  - id: person.h_edu_llc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 31 (lifetime learning credit) completed for this student"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_base, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}

  - id: person.h_edu_adj_sum
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted Qualified Education Expenses Worksheet, line 3: total adjustments"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.edu_a_this_year, person.edu_a_next_year, person.edu_refunds]}

  - id: person.h_edu_over_refused
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Assistance or refunds exceed an academic period's expenses (per-period allocation not supported)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [person.h_edu_adj_sum, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_over, {const: true}]}

  - id: person.h_edu_aoc_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualified education expenses for the American opportunity credit (tuition, fees and course materials)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.edu_inst, person.edu_mat]}

  - id: person.h_edu_aoc_adj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (AOC): line 1 minus line 3, if zero or less enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_edu_aoc_total, take: person.h_edu_adj_sum}

  - id: person.h_aoc_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.expense_cap}

  - id: person.h_edu_aoc_capped
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses, not more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_edu_aoc_adj, person.h_aoc_cap]}

  - id: person.edu_line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Don't enter more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_edu_aoc_capped}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}, then: person.h_zero}
    refuses: [person.h_edu_over_refused]
    shown_when: person.h_edu_aoc

  - id: person.h_aoc_band
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$2,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.full_band}

  - id: person.edu_line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Subtract $2,000 from line 27. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.edu_line27, take: person.h_aoc_band}
    shown_when: person.h_edu_aoc

  - id: person.edu_line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Multiply line 28 by 25% (0.25)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.edu_line28, rate: {param: aoc.second_rate}}
    shown_when: person.h_edu_aoc

  - id: person.h_line29_plus
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Add $2,000 to the amount on line 29"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.edu_line29, person.h_aoc_band]}

  - id: person.edu_line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "If line 28 is zero, enter the amount from line 27. Otherwise, add $2,000 to the amount on line 29 and enter the result"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line28, {const: 0}]}, then: person.edu_line27}
        - {guard: {op: test, gt: [person.edu_line28, {const: 0}]}, then: person.h_line29_plus}
    shown_when: person.h_edu_aoc

  - id: person.h_edu_llc_adj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (LLC): expenses required to be paid to the institution minus adjustments, if zero or less enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.edu_inst, take: person.h_edu_adj_sum}

  - id: person.edu_line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_llc, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_edu_llc_adj}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_llc, {const: false}]}, then: person.h_zero}
    refuses: [person.h_edu_over_refused]
    shown_when: person.h_edu_llc

  - id: person.edu_kid
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8863 line 7 conditions: under 18; or 18, or a full-time student over 18 and under 24, with earned income less than half of support; a parent alive at the end of 2025; not filing jointly"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.h_under18, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [person.under24, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_half_support, {const: true}]}
                - op: test
                  any:
                    - {op: test, eq: [person.h_under19, {const: true}]}
                    - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_fulltime, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_parent, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}

  # ---------------- Form 5329 screen ----------------
  - id: person.x_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 5329 situations outside the tool (education or ABLE account additional tax or excess, Archer MSA excess, prior-year IRA or HSA excess, missed required minimum distribution)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.x_edu_account, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [person.x_edu_taxable, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [person.x_edu_excess, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.x_archer_excess, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.x_ira_prior_excess, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.x_hsa_prior_excess, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.rmd_missed, {const: true}]}
    note: "Open: the pages do not name which Form 5329 parts are unsupported. Chosen: Parts II, V, VI, VIII, IX and the prior-year lines of Parts III, IV and VII; person.inherited_account is not included."

  # ---------------- Schedule 1-A Part V ----------------
  - id: person.senior_work_ssn
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You were born before January 2, 1961, and have a valid SSN (Schedule 1-A, Part V)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.role, {const: "taxpayer"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.age65, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.work_ssn, {const: true}]}

  - id: person.spouse_senior_work_ssn
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Your spouse was born before January 2, 1961, and has a valid SSN (Schedule 1-A, Part V)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.role, {const: "spouse"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.age65, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.work_ssn, {const: true}]}
```
